Implement the following plan:

# ENT-259: Fix attribution in deferred condensation

## Context

When the agent commits mid-turn (ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED), PostCommit migration updates `state.BaseCommit` to the new HEAD. Later when `handleTurnEndCondense` runs at Stop, `calculateSessionAttributions` uses `state.BaseCommit` for the base tree. Since `state.BaseCommit` now equals HEAD, `baseTree == headTree` and the diff is zero — attribution shows no changes.

The root cause: `BaseCommit` serves two purposes (shadow branch naming + attribution reference) but they diverge during deferred condensation. Migration must update it for shadow branch lookup, but attribution needs the original value.

## Plan

### 1. Add `AttributionBaseCommit` field to `session.State`

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go`

Add after `BaseCommit`:
```go
// AttributionBaseCommit is the commit used as the reference point for attribution calculations.
// Unlike BaseCommit (which tracks the shadow branch and moves with migration), this field
// preserves the original base commit so deferred condensation can correctly calculate
// agent vs human line attribution. Updated only after successful condensation.
AttributionBaseCommit string `json:"attribution_base_commit,omitempty"`
```

### 2. Set `AttributionBaseCommit` at session initialization

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go:220`

In `initializeSession`, set `AttributionBaseCommit` alongside `BaseCommit`:
```go
state := &SessionState{
    ...
    BaseCommit:            head.Hash().String(),
    AttributionBaseCommit: head.Hash().String(), // NEW
    ...
}
```

### 3. Update `AttributionBaseCommit` after successful condensation

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `condenseAndUpdateState()`

After the existing `state.BaseCommit = head.Hash().String()` (line ~720), add:
```go
state.AttributionBaseCommit = head.Hash().String()
```

This ensures the attribution base moves forward only after condensation captures the data.

### 4. Use `AttributionBaseCommit` in `calculateSessionAttributions`

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` — `calculateSessionAttributions()`

Change line ~231 from:
```go
if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(state.BaseCommit)); baseErr == nil {
```
to:
```go
attrBase := state.AttributionBaseCommit
if attrBase == "" {
    attrBase = state.BaseCommit // backward compat
}
if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(attrBase)); baseErr == nil {
```

### 5. Write a test for deferred condensation attribution

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go`

Extend `TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession` (or add a new test) to verify:
- After PostCommit migration, `state.BaseCommit != state.AttributionBaseCommit`
- After deferred condensation, the attribution in the checkpoint metadata has non-zero agent lines
- After condensation completes, `state.AttributionBaseCommit` is updated to match `state.BaseCommit`

## Key files to modify

1. `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add field
2. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go` — Set at init
3. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — Update after condensation
4. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` — Use for attribution
5. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go` — Test

## Verification

```bash
mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci
```

Also verify `TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession` specifically passes with attribution verification.

   112→// PromptAttribution captures line-level attribution data at the start of each prompt.
   113→// By recording what changed since the last checkpoint BEFORE the agent works,
   114→// we can accurately separate user edits from agent contributions.
   115→type PromptAttribution struct {
   116→	// CheckpointNumber is which checkpoint this was recorded before (1-indexed)
   117→	CheckpointNumber int `json:"checkpoint_number"`
   118→
   119→	// UserLinesAdded is lines added by user since the last checkpoint
   120→	UserLinesAdded int `json:"user_lines_added"`
   121→
   122→	// UserLinesRemoved is lines removed by user since the last checkpoint
   123→	UserLinesRemoved int `json:"user_lines_removed"`
   124→
   125→	// AgentLinesAdded is total agent lines added so far (base → last checkpoint).
   126→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   127→	AgentLinesAdded int `json:"agent_lines_added"`
   128→
   129→	// AgentLinesRemoved is total agent lines removed so far (base → last checkpoint).
   130→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   131→	AgentLinesRemoved int `json:"agent_lines_removed"`
   132→
   133→	// UserAddedPerFile tracks per-file user additions for accurate modification tracking.
   134→	// This enables distinguishing user self-modifications from agent modifications.
   135→	// See docs/architecture/attribution.md for details.
   136→	UserAddedPerFile map[string]int `json:"user_added_per_file,omitempty"`
   137→}
   138→
   139→// NormalizeAfterLoad applies backward-compatible migrations to state loaded from disk.
   140→// Call this after deserializing a State from JSON.
   141→func (s *State) NormalizeAfterLoad() {
   142→	// Migrate transcript fields: CheckpointTranscriptStart replaces both
   143→	// CondensedTranscriptLines and TranscriptLinesAtStart from older state files.
   144→	if s.CheckpointTranscriptStart == 0 {
   145→		if s.CondensedTranscriptLines > 0 {
   146→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.CondensedTranscriptLines
   147→		} else if s.TranscriptLinesAtStart > 0 {
   148→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.TranscriptLinesAtStart
   149→		}
   150→	}
   151→	// Clear deprecated fields so they aren't re-persisted.
   152→	// Note: this is a one-way migration. If the state is re-saved, older CLI versions
   153→	// will see 0 for these fields and fall back to scoping from the transcript start.
   154→	// This is acceptable since CLI upgrades are monotonic and the worst case is
   155→	// redundant transcript content in a condensation, not data loss.
   156→	s.CondensedTranscriptLines = 0
   157→	s.TranscriptLinesAtStart = 0
   158→}
   159→
   160→// StateStore provides low-level operations for managing session state files.
   161→//
   162→// StateStore is a primitive for session state persistence. It is NOT the same as
   163→// the Sessions interface - it only handles state files in .git/entire-sessions/,
   164→// not the full session data which includes checkpoint content.
   165→//
   166→// Use StateStore directly in strategies for performance-critical state operations.
   167→// Use the Sessions interface (when implemented) for high-level session management.
   168→type StateStore struct {
   169→	// stateDir is the directory where session state files are stored
   170→	stateDir string
   171→}
   172→
   173→// NewStateStore creates a new state store.
   174→// Uses the git common dir to store session state (shared across worktrees).
   175→func NewStateStore() (*StateStore, error) {
   176→	commonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()
   177→	if err != nil {
   178→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   179→	}
   180→	return &StateStore{
   181→		stateDir: filepath.Join(commonDir, SessionStateDirName),
   182→	}, nil
   183→}
   184→
   185→// NewStateStoreWithDir creates a new state store with a custom directory.
   186→// This is useful for testing.
   187→func NewStateStoreWithDir(stateDir string) *StateStore {
   188→	return &StateStore{stateDir: stateDir}
   189→}
   190→
   191→// Load loads the session state for the given session ID.
   192→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
   193→func (s *StateStore) Load(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) (*State, error) {
   194→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   195→
   196→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   197→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   198→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   202→
   203→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID
   204→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   205→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
   206→	}
   207→	if err != nil {
   208→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	var state State
   212→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   213→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
   214→	}
   215→	state.NormalizeAfterLoad()
   216→	return &state, nil
   217→}
   218→
   219→// Save saves the session state atomically.
   220→func (s *StateStore) Save(ctx context.Context, state *State) error {
   221→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   222→
   223→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   224→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
   225→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	if err := os.MkdirAll(s.stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   229→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   233→	if err != nil {
   234→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(state.SessionID)
   238→
   239→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
   240→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
   241→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   242→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
   243→	}
   244→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
   245→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
   246→	}
   247→	return nil
   248→}
   249→
   250→// Clear removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   251→func (s *StateStore) Clear(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) error {
   252→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   253→
   254→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   255→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   256→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   260→
   261→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   262→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   263→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   264→		}
   265→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   266→	}
   267→	return nil
   268→}
   269→
   270→// RemoveAll removes the entire session state directory.
   271→// This is used during uninstall to completely remove all session state.
   272→func (s *StateStore) RemoveAll() error {
   273→	if err := os.RemoveAll(s.stateDir); err != nil {
   274→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state directory: %w", err)
   275→	}
   276→	return nil
   277→}
   278→
   279→// List returns all session states.
   280→func (s *StateStore) List(ctx context.Context) ([]*State, error) {
   281→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   282→
   283→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(s.stateDir)
   284→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   285→		return nil, nil
   286→	}
   287→	if err != nil {
   288→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	var states []*State
   292→	for _, entry := range entries {
   293→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
   294→			continue
   295→		}
   296→		if strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".tmp") {
   297→			continue // Skip temp files
   298→		}
   299→
   300→		sessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
   301→		state, err := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   302→		if err != nil {
   303→			continue // Skip corrupted state files
   304→		}
   305→		if state == nil {
   306→			continue
   307→		}
   308→
   309→		states = append(states, state)
   310→	}
   311→	return states, nil
   312→}
   313→
   314→// stateFilePath returns the path to a session state file.
   315→func (s *StateStore) stateFilePath(sessionID string) string {
   316→	return filepath.Join(s.stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   317→}
   318→
   319→// getGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   320→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   321→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   322→func getGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   323→	ctx := context.Background()
   324→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   325→	cmd.Dir = "."
   326→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   327→	if err != nil {
   328→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   329→	}
   330→
   331→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   332→
   333→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   334→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   335→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   336→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   340→}

    16→// Shadow strategy session state methods.
    17→// Uses session.StateStore for persistence.
    18→
    19→// loadSessionState loads session state using the StateStore.
    20→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) loadSessionState(sessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
    21→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		return nil, err
    24→	}
    25→	state, err := store.Load(context.Background(), sessionID)
    26→	if err != nil {
    27→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→	return state, nil
    30→}
    31→
    32→// saveSessionState saves session state using the StateStore.
    33→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) saveSessionState(state *SessionState) error {
    34→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return err
    37→	}
    38→	if err := store.Save(context.Background(), state); err != nil {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}
    43→
    44→// clearSessionState clears session state using the StateStore.
    45→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) clearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
    46→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		return err
    49→	}
    50→	if err := store.Clear(context.Background(), sessionID); err != nil {
    51→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→	return nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→// listAllSessionStates returns all active session states.
    57→// It filters out orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists.
    58→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listAllSessionStates() ([]*SessionState, error) {
    59→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get state store: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	sessionStates, err := store.List(context.Background())
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	if len(sessionStates) == 0 {
    70→		return nil, nil
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	var states []*SessionState
    79→	for _, sessionState := range sessionStates {
    80→		state := sessionState
    81→
    82→		// Skip and cleanup orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists.
    83→		// Keep active sessions (shadow branch may not be created yet) and sessions
    84→		// with LastCheckpointID (needed for checkpoint ID reuse on subsequent commits).
    85→		// Clean up everything else: stale pre-state-machine sessions (empty phase),
    86→		// IDLE/ENDED sessions that were never condensed, etc.
    87→		shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    88→		refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    89→		if _, err := repo.Reference(refName, true); err != nil {
    90→			if !state.Phase.IsActive() && state.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
    91→				//nolint:errcheck,gosec // G104: Cleanup is best-effort, shouldn't fail the list operation
    92→				store.Clear(context.Background(), state.SessionID)
    93→				continue
    94→			}
    95→		}
    96→
    97→		states = append(states, state)
    98→	}
    99→	return states, nil
   100→}
   101→
   102→// findSessionsForWorktree finds all sessions for the given worktree path.
   103→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   104→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   105→	if err != nil {
   106→		return nil, err
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	var matching []*SessionState
   110→	for _, state := range allStates {
   111→		if state.WorktreePath == worktreePath {
   112→			matching = append(matching, state)
   113→		}
   114→	}
   115→	return matching, nil
   116→}
   117→
   118→// findSessionsForCommit finds all sessions where base_commit matches the given SHA.
   119→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   120→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   121→	if err != nil {
   122→		return nil, err
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	var matching []*SessionState
   126→	for _, state := range allStates {
   127→		if state.BaseCommit == baseCommitSHA {
   128→			matching = append(matching, state)
   129→		}
   130→	}
   131→	return matching, nil
   132→}
   133→
   134→// FindSessionsForCommit is the exported version of findSessionsForCommit.
   135→// Used by the rewind reset command to find sessions to clean up.
   136→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) FindSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   137→	return s.findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA)
   138→}
   139→
   140→// ClearSessionState is the exported version of clearSessionState.
   141→// Used by the rewind reset command to clean up session state files.
   142→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) ClearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
   143→	return s.clearSessionState(sessionID)
   144→}
   145→
   146→// CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints counts how many other active sessions
   147→// from the SAME worktree (different from currentSessionID) have created checkpoints
   148→// on the SAME base commit (current HEAD). This is used to show an informational message
   149→// about concurrent sessions that will be included in the next commit.
   150→// Returns 0, nil if no such sessions exist.
   151→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (int, error) {
   152→	currentWorktree, err := GetWorktreePath()
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		return 0, err
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	// Get current HEAD to compare with session base commits
   158→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return 0, err
   161→	}
   162→	head, err := repo.Head()
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   165→	}
   166→	currentHead := head.Hash().String()
   167→
   168→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return 0, err
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	count := 0
   174→	for _, state := range allStates {
   175→		// Only consider sessions from the same worktree with checkpoints
   176→		// AND based on the same commit (current HEAD)
   177→		// Sessions from different base commits are independent and shouldn't be counted
   178→		if state.SessionID != currentSessionID &&
   179→			state.WorktreePath == currentWorktree &&
   180→			state.StepCount > 0 &&
   181→			state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
   182→			count++
   183→		}
   184→	}
   185→	return count, nil
   186→}
   187→
   188→// initializeSession creates a new session state or updates a partial one.
   189→// A partial state may exist if the concurrent session warning was shown.
   190→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   191→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   192→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   193→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) initializeSession(repo *git.Repository, sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) (*SessionState, error) {
   194→	head, err := repo.Head()
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Get worktree ID for shadow branch naming
   205→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree ID: %w", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Capture untracked files at session start to preserve them during rewind
   211→	untrackedFiles, err := collectUntrackedFiles()
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		// Non-fatal: continue even if we can't collect untracked files
   214→		untrackedFiles = nil
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	now := time.Now()
   218→	state := &SessionState{
   219→		SessionID:             sessionID,
   220→		BaseCommit:            head.Hash().String(),
   221→		WorktreePath:          worktreePath,
   222→		WorktreeID:            worktreeID,
   223→		StartedAt:             now,
   224→		LastInteractionTime:   &now,
   225→		StepCount:             0,
   226→		UntrackedFilesAtStart: untrackedFiles,
   227→		AgentType:             agentType,
   228→		TranscriptPath:        transcriptPath,
   229→		FirstPrompt:           truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt),
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   233→		return nil, err
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	return state, nil
   237→}
   238→
   239→// getShadowBranchNameForCommit returns the shadow branch name for the given base commit and worktree ID.
   240→// worktreeID should be empty for the main worktree or the internal git worktree name for linked worktrees.
   241→func getShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID string) string {
   242→	return checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   243→}

    28→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the sessions branch.
    29→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/sessions.
    30→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    31→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    34→	}
    35→
    36→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    37→	if err != nil {
    38→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    42→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    43→	for _, c := range committed {
    44→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    45→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    46→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    47→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    48→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    49→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    50→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    51→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    52→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    53→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    54→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    55→		})
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	return result, nil
    59→}
    60→
    61→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    62→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    63→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		return nil, err
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    69→	for _, cp := range all {
    70→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    71→			result = append(result, cp)
    72→		}
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	if len(result) == 0 {
    76→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	return result, nil
    80→}
    81→
    82→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    83→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/sessions.
    84→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    85→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    91→	if err != nil {
    92→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    93→	}
    94→	if content == nil {
    95→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    96→	}
    97→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
    98→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	return content.Transcript, nil
   102→}
   103→
   104→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   105→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   106→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
   107→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteCommitted for the git operations.
   108→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   109→	// Get shadow branch
   110→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   111→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   112→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("shadow branch not found: %w", err)
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	// Extract session data from the shadow branch (with live transcript fallback).
   118→	// Use tracked files from session state instead of collecting all files from tree.
   119→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini).
   120→	// Pass live transcript path so condensation reads the current file rather than a
   121→	// potentially stale shadow branch copy (SaveChanges may have been skipped if the
   122→	// last turn had no code changes).
   123→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath)
   124→	if err != nil {
   125→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Get checkpoint store
   129→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   130→	if err != nil {
   131→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	// Get author info
   135→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   136→	attribution := calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
   137→	// Get current branch name
   138→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   139→
   140→	// Generate summary if enabled
   141→	var summary *cpkg.Summary
   142→	if settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() && len(sessionData.Transcript) > 0 {
   143→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   144→		summarizeCtx := logging.WithComponent(logCtx, "summarize")
   145→
   146→		// Scope transcript to this checkpoint's portion
   147→		scopedTranscript := transcript.SliceFromLine(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   148→		if len(scopedTranscript) > 0 {
   149→			var err error
   150→			summary, err = summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(summarizeCtx, scopedTranscript, sessionData.FilesTouched, nil)
   151→			if err != nil {
   152→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed",
   153→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   154→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   155→				// Continue without summary - non-blocking
   156→			} else {
   157→				logging.Info(summarizeCtx, "summary generated",
   158→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
   159→			}
   160→		}
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	// Write checkpoint metadata using the checkpoint store
   164→	if err := store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), cpkg.WriteCommittedOptions{
   165→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   166→		SessionID:                   state.SessionID,
   167→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   168→		Branch:                      branchName,
   169→		Transcript:                  sessionData.Transcript,
   170→		Prompts:                     sessionData.Prompts,
   171→		Context:                     sessionData.Context,
   172→		FilesTouched:                sessionData.FilesTouched,
   173→		CheckpointsCount:            state.StepCount,
   174→		EphemeralBranch:             shadowBranchName,
   175→		AuthorName:                  authorName,
   176→		AuthorEmail:                 authorEmail,
   177→		Agent:                       state.AgentType,
   178→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   179→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   state.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   180→		TokenUsage:                  sessionData.TokenUsage,
   181→		InitialAttribution:          attribution,
   182→		Summary:                     summary,
   183→	}); err != nil {
   184→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint metadata: %w", err)
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	return &CondenseResult{
   188→		CheckpointID:         checkpointID,
   189→		SessionID:            state.SessionID,
   190→		CheckpointsCount:     state.StepCount,
   191→		FilesTouched:         sessionData.FilesTouched,
   192→		TotalTranscriptLines: sessionData.FullTranscriptLines,
   193→	}, nil
   194→}
   195→
   196→func calculateSessionAttributions(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef *plumbing.Reference, sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, state *SessionState) *cpkg.InitialAttribution {
   197→	// Calculate initial attribution using accumulated prompt attribution data.
   198→	// This uses user edits captured at each prompt start (before agent works),
   199→	// plus any user edits after the final checkpoint (shadow → head).
   200→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   201→	var attribution *cpkg.InitialAttribution
   202→	headRef, headErr := repo.Head()
   203→	if headErr != nil {
   204→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD",
   205→			slog.String("error", headErr.Error()))
   206→	} else {
   207→		headCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
   208→		if commitErr != nil {
   209→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD commit",
   210→				slog.String("error", commitErr.Error()))
   211→		} else {
   212→			headTree, treeErr := headCommit.Tree()
   213→			if treeErr != nil {
   214→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD tree",
   215→					slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
   216→			} else {
   217→				// Get shadow branch tree (checkpoint tree - what the agent wrote)
   218→				shadowCommit, shadowErr := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   219→				if shadowErr != nil {
   220→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow commit",
   221→						slog.String("error", shadowErr.Error()),
   222→						slog.String("shadow_ref", shadowRef.Hash().String()))
   223→				} else {
   224→					shadowTree, shadowTreeErr := shadowCommit.Tree()
   225→					if shadowTreeErr != nil {
   226→						logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow tree",
   227→							slog.String("error", shadowTreeErr.Error()))
   228→					} else {
   229→						// Get base tree (state before session started)
   230→						var baseTree *object.Tree
   231→						if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(state.BaseCommit)); baseErr == nil {
   232→							if tree, baseTErr := baseCommit.Tree(); baseTErr == nil {
   233→								baseTree = tree
   234→							} else {
   235→								logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base tree unavailable",
   236→									slog.String("error", baseTErr.Error()))
   237→							}
   238→						} else {
   239→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base commit unavailable",
   240→								slog.String("error", baseErr.Error()),
   241→								slog.String("base_commit", state.BaseCommit))
   242→						}
   243→
   244→						// Log accumulated prompt attributions for debugging
   245→						var totalUserAdded, totalUserRemoved int
   246→						for i, pa := range state.PromptAttributions {
   247→							totalUserAdded += pa.UserLinesAdded
   248→							totalUserRemoved += pa.UserLinesRemoved
   249→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution data",
   250→								slog.Int("checkpoint", pa.CheckpointNumber),
   251→								slog.Int("user_added", pa.UserLinesAdded),
   252→								slog.Int("user_removed", pa.UserLinesRemoved),
   253→								slog.Int("agent_added", pa.AgentLinesAdded),
   254→								slog.Int("agent_removed", pa.AgentLinesRemoved),
   255→								slog.Int("index", i))
   256→						}
   257→
   258→						attribution = CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   259→							baseTree,
   260→							shadowTree,
   261→							headTree,
   262→							sessionData.FilesTouched,
   263→							state.PromptAttributions,
   264→						)
   265→
   266→						if attribution != nil {
   267→							logging.Info(logCtx, "attribution calculated",
   268→								slog.Int("agent_lines", attribution.AgentLines),
   269→								slog.Int("human_added", attribution.HumanAdded),
   270→								slog.Int("human_modified", attribution.HumanModified),
   271→								slog.Int("human_removed", attribution.HumanRemoved),
   272→								slog.Int("total_committed", attribution.TotalCommitted),
   273→								slog.Float64("agent_percentage", attribution.AgentPercentage),
   274→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_added", totalUserAdded),
   275→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_removed", totalUserRemoved),
   276→								slog.Int("files_touched", len(sessionData.FilesTouched)))
   277→						}
   278→					}
   279→				}
   280→			}
   281→		}
   282→	}
   283→	return attribution
   284→}
   285→
   286→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   287→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   288→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   289→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   290→// This handles the case where SaveChanges was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   291→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   292→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   293→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)
   294→	if err != nil {
   295→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   299→	if err != nil {
   300→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   304→	// sessionID is already an "entire session ID" (with date prefix)
   305→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   306→
   307→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   308→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   309→	// (SaveChanges is only called when there are file modifications).
   310→	var fullTranscript string
   311→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   312→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   313→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   314→		}
   315→	}
   316→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   317→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   318→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   319→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   320→				fullTranscript = content
   321→			}
   322→		} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   323→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   324→				fullTranscript = content
   325→			}
   326→		}
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	// Process transcript based on agent type
   330→	if fullTranscript != "" {
   331→		// Check if this is a Gemini CLI transcript (JSON format, not JSONL)
   332→		isGeminiFormat := agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || isGeminiJSONTranscript(fullTranscript)
   333→
   334→		if isGeminiFormat {
   335→			// Gemini uses JSON format with a "messages" array
   336→			data.Transcript = []byte(fullTranscript)
   337→			data.FullTranscriptLines = 1 // JSON is a single "line"
   338→			data.Prompts = extractUserPromptsFromGeminiJSON(fullTranscript)
   339→			data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   340→		} else {
   341→			// Claude Code and others use JSONL format (one JSON object per line)
   342→			allLines := strings.Split(fullTranscript, "\n")
   343→
   344→			// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   345→			for len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == "" {
   346→				allLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]
   347→			}
   348→
   349→			data.FullTranscriptLines = len(allLines)
   350→
   351→			// Always store the full transcript for complete session history
   352→			data.Transcript = []byte(strings.Join(allLines, "\n"))
   353→
   354→			// Extract prompts from the full transcript
   355→			data.Prompts = extractUserPromptsFromLines(allLines)
   356→
   357→			// Generate context from prompts
   358→			data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   359→		}
   360→	}
   361→
   362→	// Use tracked files from session state (not all files in tree)
   363→	data.FilesTouched = filesTouched
   364→
   365→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   366→	// TODO: Missing Gemini token usage
   367→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   368→		// TODO: Calculate token usage per transcript slice (only checkpoint related)
   369→		transcriptLines, err := claudecode.ParseTranscript(data.Transcript)
   370→		if err == nil && len(transcriptLines) > 0 {
   371→			data.TokenUsage = claudecode.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptLines)
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return data, nil
   376→}
   377→
   378→// isGeminiJSONTranscript detects if the transcript is in Gemini's JSON format.
   379→// Gemini transcripts start with a JSON object containing a "messages" array.
   380→func isGeminiJSONTranscript(content string) bool {
   381→	content = strings.TrimSpace(content)
   382→	// Quick check: Gemini JSON starts with { and contains "messages"
   383→	if !strings.HasPrefix(content, "{") {
   384→		return false
   385→	}
   386→	// Try to parse as Gemini format
   387→	var transcript struct {
   388→		Messages []json.RawMessage `json:"messages"`
   389→	}
   390→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &transcript); err != nil {
   391→		return false
   392→	}
   393→	return len(transcript.Messages) > 0
   394→}
   395→
   396→// extractUserPromptsFromGeminiJSON extracts user prompts from Gemini's JSON transcript format.
   397→// Gemini transcripts are structured as: {"messages": [{"type": "user", "content": "..."}, ...]}
   398→func extractUserPromptsFromGeminiJSON(content string) []string {
   399→	var transcript struct {
   400→		Messages []struct {
   401→			Type    string `json:"type"`
   402→			Content string `json:"content"`
   403→		} `json:"messages"`
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &transcript); err != nil {
   407→		return nil
   408→	}
   409→
   410→	var prompts []string
   411→	for _, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   412→		if msg.Type == "user" && msg.Content != "" {
   413→			// Strip IDE context tags for consistency with Claude Code handling
   414→			cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(msg.Content)
   415→			if cleaned != "" {
   416→				prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   417→			}
   418→		}
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	return prompts
   422→}
   423→
   424→// extractUserPromptsFromLines extracts user prompts from JSONL transcript lines.
   425→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   426→func extractUserPromptsFromLines(lines []string) []string {
   427→	var prompts []string
   428→	for _, line := range lines {
   429→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   430→		if line == "" {
   431→			continue
   432→		}
   433→
   434→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   435→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   436→			continue
   437→		}
   438→
   439→		// Check for user message (supports both "human" and "user" types)
   440→		msgType, ok := entry["type"].(string)
   441→		if !ok || (msgType != "human" && msgType != "user") {
   442→			continue
   443→		}
   444→
   445→		// Extract message content
   446→		message, ok := entry["message"].(map[string]interface{})
   447→		if !ok {
   448→			continue
   449→		}
   450→
   451→		// Handle string content
   452→		if content, ok := message["content"].(string); ok && content != "" {
   453→			cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content)
   454→			if cleaned != "" {
   455→				prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   456→			}
   457→			continue
   458→		}
   459→
   460→		// Handle array content (e.g., multiple text blocks from VSCode)
   461→		if arr, ok := message["content"].([]interface{}); ok {
   462→			var texts []string
   463→			for _, item := range arr {
   464→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   465→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   466→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   467→							texts = append(texts, text)
   468→						}
   469→					}
   470→				}
   471→			}
   472→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   473→				cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   474→				if cleaned != "" {
   475→					prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   476→				}
   477→			}
   478→		}
   479→	}
   480→	return prompts
   481→}
   482→
   483→// generateContextFromPrompts generates context.md content from a list of prompts.
   484→func generateContextFromPrompts(prompts []string) []byte {
   485→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   486→		return nil
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	var buf strings.Builder
   490→	buf.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   491→	buf.WriteString("## User Prompts\n\n")
   492→
   493→	for i, prompt := range prompts {
   494→		// Truncate very long prompts for readability
   495→		displayPrompt := prompt
   496→		if len(displayPrompt) > 500 {
   497→			displayPrompt = displayPrompt[:500] + "..."
   498→		}
   499→		buf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n", i+1))
   500→		buf.WriteString(displayPrompt)
   501→		buf.WriteString("\n\n")
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	return []byte(buf.String())
   505→}
   506→
   507→// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session by its ID and cleans up.
   508→// This is used by "entire sessions fix" to salvage stuck sessions.
   509→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error {
   510→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "condense-by-id")
   511→
   512→	// Load session state
   513→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   514→	if err != nil {
   515→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   516→	}
   517→	if state == nil {
   518→		return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   519→	}
   520→
   521→	// Open repository
   522→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   523→	if err != nil {
   524→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	// Generate a checkpoint ID
   528→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   529→	if err != nil {
   530→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   531→	}
   532→
   533→	// Check if shadow branch exists (required for condensation)
   534→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   535→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   536→	_, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   537→	hasShadowBranch := refErr == nil
   538→
   539→	if !hasShadowBranch {
   540→		// No shadow branch means no checkpoint data to condense.
   541→		// Just clean up the state file.
   542→		logging.Info(ctx, "no shadow branch for session, clearing state only",
   543→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   544→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   545→		)
   546→		if err := s.clearSessionState(sessionID); err != nil {
   547→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   548→		}
   549→		return nil
   550→	}
   551→
   552→	// Condense the session
   553→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state)
   554→	if err != nil {
   555→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to condense session: %w", err)
   556→	}
   557→
   558→	logging.Info(ctx, "session condensed by ID",
   559→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   560→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   561→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   562→	)
   563→
   564→	// Update session state: reset step count and transition to idle
   565→	state.StepCount = 0
   566→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   567→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   568→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   569→	state.PendingCheckpointID = checkpointID.String()
   570→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   571→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   572→
   573→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   574→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   575→	}
   576→
   577→	// Clean up shadow branch if no other sessions need it
   578→	if err := s.cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo, shadowBranchName, sessionID); err != nil {
   579→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to clean up shadow branch",
   580→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   581→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   582→		)
   583→		// Non-fatal: condensation succeeded, shadow branch cleanup is best-effort
   584→	}
   585→
   586→	return nil
   587→}
   588→
   589→// cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused deletes a shadow branch if no other active sessions reference it.
   590→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo *git.Repository, shadowBranchName, excludeSessionID string) error {
   591→	// List all session states to check if any other session uses this shadow branch
   592→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   593→	if err != nil {
   594→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   595→	}
   596→
   597→	for _, state := range allStates {
   598→		if state.SessionID == excludeSessionID {
   599→			continue
   600→		}
   601→		otherShadow := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   602→		if otherShadow == shadowBranchName && state.StepCount > 0 {
   603→			// Another session still needs this shadow branch
   604→			return nil
   605→		}
   606→	}
   607→
   608→	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch
   609→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   610→	ref, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   611→	if refErr != nil {
   612→		// Branch already gone, nothing to clean up
   613→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // intentional: missing branch is not an error
   614→	}
   615→	if err := repo.Storer.RemoveReference(ref.Name()); err != nil {
   616→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch reference: %w", err)
   617→	}
   618→	return nil
   619→}
