    27→
    28→// askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
    29→// This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
    30→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
    31→// If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
    32→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    33→	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
    34→	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
    35→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		// Can't open TTY (e.g., running in CI), use default
    38→		return defaultYes
    39→	}
    40→	defer tty.Close()
    41→
    42→	// Show context if provided
    43→	if context != "" {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s\n", context)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	// Show prompt with default indicator
    48→	// Write to tty directly, not stderr, since git hooks may redirect stderr to /dev/null
    49→	var hint string
    50→	if defaultYes {
    51→		hint = "[Y/n]"
    52→	} else {
    53→		hint = "[y/N]"
    54→	}
    55→	fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s %s ", prompt, hint)
    56→
    57→	// Read response
    58→	reader := bufio.NewReader(tty)
    59→	response, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return defaultYes
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	response = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(response))
    65→	switch response {
    66→	case "y", "yes":
    67→		return true
    68→	case "n", "no":
    69→		return false
    70→	default:
    71→		// Empty or invalid input - use default
    72→		return defaultYes
    73→	}
    74→}
    75→
    76→// CommitMsg is called by the git commit-msg hook after the user edits the message.
    77→// If the message contains only our trailer (no actual user content), strip it
    78→// so git will abort the commit due to empty message.
    79→//
    80→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
    81→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CommitMsg(commitMsgFile string) error {
    82→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from git hook
    83→	if err != nil {
    84→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	message := string(content)
    88→
    89→	// Check if our trailer is present (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
    90→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); !found {
    91→		// No trailer, nothing to do
    92→		return nil
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Check if there's any user content (non-comment, non-trailer lines)
    96→	if !hasUserContent(message) {
    97→		// No user content - strip the trailer so git aborts
    98→		message = stripCheckpointTrailer(message)
    99→		if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   100→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   101→		}
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	return nil
   105→}
   106→
   107→// hasUserContent checks if the message has any content besides comments and our trailer.
   108→func hasUserContent(message string) bool {
   109→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   110→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   111→		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
   112→		// Skip empty lines
   113→		if trimmed == "" {
   114→			continue
   115→		}
   116→		// Skip comment lines
   117→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "#") {
   118→			continue
   119→		}
   120→		// Skip our trailer line
   121→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, trailerPrefix) {
   122→			continue
   123→		}
   124→		// Found user content
   125→		return true
   126→	}
   127→	return false
   128→}
   129→
   130→// stripCheckpointTrailer removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer line from the message.
   131→func stripCheckpointTrailer(message string) string {
   132→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   133→	var result []string
   134→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   135→		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(line), trailerPrefix) {
   136→			result = append(result, line)
   137→		}
   138→	}
   139→	return strings.Join(result, "\n")
   140→}
   141→
   142→// isGitSequenceOperation checks if git is currently in the middle of a rebase,
   143→// cherry-pick, or revert operation. During these operations, commits are being
   144→// replayed and should not be linked to agent sessions.
   145→//
   146→// Detects:
   147→//   - rebase: .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ directories
   148→//   - cherry-pick: .git/CHERRY_PICK_HEAD file
   149→//   - revert: .git/REVERT_HEAD file
   150→func isGitSequenceOperation() bool {
   151→	// Get git directory (handles worktrees and relative paths correctly)
   152→	gitDir, err := GetGitDir()
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		return false // Can't determine, assume not in sequence operation
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	// Check for rebase state directories
   158→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")); err == nil {
   159→		return true
   160→	}
   161→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-apply")); err == nil {
   162→		return true
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Check for cherry-pick and revert state files
   166→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "CHERRY_PICK_HEAD")); err == nil {
   167→		return true
   168→	}
   169→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "REVERT_HEAD")); err == nil {
   170→		return true
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	return false
   174→}
   175→
   176→// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   177→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer to the commit message with a stable checkpoint ID.
   178→// Only adds a trailer if there's actually new session content to condense.
   179→// The actual condensation happens in PostCommit - if the user removes the trailer,
   180→// the commit will not be linked to the session (useful for "manual" commits).
   181→// For amended commits, preserves the existing checkpoint ID.
   182→//
   183→// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   184→//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow - adds trailer with explanatory comment
   185→//   - "message": using -m or -F flag - prompts user interactively via /dev/tty
   186→//   - "merge", "squash": skip trailer entirely (auto-generated messages)
   187→//   - "commit": amend operation - preserves existing trailer or restores from PendingCheckpointID
   188→//
   189→
   190→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error {
   191→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   192→
   193→	// Skip during rebase, cherry-pick, or revert operations
   194→	// These are replaying existing commits and should not be linked to agent sessions
   195→	if isGitSequenceOperation() {
   196→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped during git sequence operation",
   197→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   198→			slog.String("source", source),
   199→		)
   200→		return nil
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	// Skip for merge and squash sources
   204→	// These are auto-generated messages - not from Claude sessions
   205→	switch source {
   206→	case "merge", "squash":
   207→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped for source",
   208→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   209→			slog.String("source", source),
   210→		)
   211→		return nil
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	// Handle amend (source="commit") separately: preserve or restore trailer
   215→	if source == "commit" {
   216→		return s.handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx, commitMsgFile)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   225→	if err != nil {
   226→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   230→	// We match by worktree (not BaseCommit) because the user may have made
   231→	// intermediate commits without entering new prompts, causing HEAD to diverge
   232→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   233→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   234→		// No active sessions or error listing - silently skip (hooks must be resilient)
   235→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no active sessions",
   236→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   237→			slog.String("source", source),
   238→		)
   239→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	// Check if any session has new content to condense
   243→	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
   244→
   245→	// Determine which checkpoint ID to use
   246→	var checkpointID id.CheckpointID
   247→	var hasNewContent bool
   248→	var reusedSession *SessionState
   249→
   250→	if len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   251→		// New content exists - will generate new checkpoint ID below
   252→		hasNewContent = true
   253→	} else {
   254→		// No new content - check if any session has a LastCheckpointID to reuse
   255→		// This handles the case where user splits Claude's work across multiple commits
   256→		// Reuse if: LastCheckpointID exists AND (FilesTouched is empty OR files overlap)
   257→		// - FilesTouched empty: commits made before session stop, reuse checkpoint
   258→		// - FilesTouched populated: only reuse if files overlap (prevents unrelated commits from reusing)
   259→
   260→		// Get current HEAD to filter sessions
   261→		head, err := repo.Head()
   262→		if err != nil {
   263→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   264→		}
   265→		currentHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   266→
   267→		// Filter to sessions where BaseCommit matches current HEAD
   268→		// This prevents reusing checkpoint IDs from old sessions
   269→		// Note: BaseCommit is kept current both when new content is condensed (in the
   270→		// condensation process) and when no new content is found (via PostCommit when
   271→		// reusing checkpoint IDs). If none match, we don't add a trailer rather than
   272→		// falling back to old sessions which could have stale checkpoint IDs.
   273→		var currentSessions []*SessionState
   274→		for _, session := range sessions {
   275→			if session.BaseCommit == currentHeadHash {
   276→				currentSessions = append(currentSessions, session)
   277→			}
   278→		}
   279→
   280→		if len(currentSessions) == 0 {
   281→			// No sessions match current HEAD - don't try to reuse checkpoint IDs
   282→			// from old sessions as they may be stale
   283→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no sessions match current HEAD",
   284→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   285→				slog.String("source", source),
   286→				slog.String("current_head", currentHeadHash[:7]),
   287→				slog.Int("total_sessions", len(sessions)),
   288→			)
   289→			return nil
   290→		}
   291→
   292→		stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
   293→		for _, session := range currentSessions {
   294→			if !session.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() &&
   295→				(len(session.FilesTouched) == 0 || hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, session.FilesTouched)) {
   296→				checkpointID = session.LastCheckpointID
   297→				reusedSession = session
   298→				break
   299→			}
   300→		}
   301→		if checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
   302→			// No new content and no previous checkpoint to reference (or staged files are unrelated)
   303→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no content to link",
   304→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   305→				slog.String("source", source),
   306→				slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
   307→				slog.Int("sessions_with_content", len(sessionsWithContent)),
   308→			)
   309→			return nil
   310→		}
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	// Read current commit message
   314→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   315→	if err != nil {
   316→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	message := string(content)
   320→
   321→	// Get or generate checkpoint ID (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   322→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   323→		// Trailer already exists (e.g., amend) - keep it
   324→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer already exists",
   325→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   326→			slog.String("source", source),
   327→			slog.String("existing_checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   328→		)
   329→		return nil
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	if hasNewContent {
   333→		// New content: check PendingCheckpointID first (set during previous condensation),
   334→		// otherwise generate a new one. This ensures idempotent IDs across hook invocations.
   335→		for _, state := range sessionsWithContent {
   336→			if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
   337→				if cpID, err := id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID); err == nil {
   338→					checkpointID = cpID
   339→					break
   340→				}
   341→			}
   342→		}
   343→		if checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
   344→			cpID, err := id.Generate()
   345→			if err != nil {
   346→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   347→			}
   348→			checkpointID = cpID
   349→		}
   350→	}
   351→	// Otherwise checkpointID is already set to LastCheckpointID from above
   352→
   353→	// Determine agent type and last prompt from session
   354→	agentType := DefaultAgentType // default for backward compatibility
   355→	var lastPrompt string
   356→	if hasNewContent && len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   357→		session := sessionsWithContent[0]
   358→		if session.AgentType != "" {
   359→			agentType = session.AgentType
   360→		}
   361→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, session)
   362→	} else if reusedSession != nil {
   363→		// Reusing checkpoint from existing session - get agent type and prompt from that session
   364→		if reusedSession.AgentType != "" {
   365→			agentType = reusedSession.AgentType
   366→		}
   367→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, reusedSession)
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	// Prepare prompt for display: collapse newlines/whitespace, then truncate (rune-safe)
   371→	displayPrompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(lastPrompt), 80, "...")
   372→
   373→	// Check if we're restoring an existing checkpoint ID (already condensed)
   374→	// vs linking a genuinely new checkpoint. Restoring doesn't need user confirmation
   375→	// since the data is already committed — this handles git commit --amend -m "..."
   376→	// and non-interactive environments (e.g., Claude doing commits).
   377→	isRestoringExisting := false
   378→	if !hasNewContent && reusedSession != nil {
   379→		// Reusing LastCheckpointID from a previous condensation
   380→		isRestoringExisting = true
   381→	} else if hasNewContent {
   382→		for _, state := range sessionsWithContent {
   383→			if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
   384→				isRestoringExisting = true
   385→				break
   386→			}
   387→		}
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Add trailer differently based on commit source
   391→	switch {
   392→	case source == "message" && !isRestoringExisting:
   393→		// Using -m or -F with genuinely new content: ask user interactively
   394→		// whether to add trailer (comments won't be stripped by git in this mode)
   395→
   396→		// Build context string for interactive prompt
   397→		var promptContext string
   398→		if displayPrompt != "" {
   399→			promptContext = "You have an active " + string(agentType) + " session.\nLast Prompt: " + displayPrompt
   400→		}
   401→
   402→		if !askConfirmTTY("Link this commit to "+string(agentType)+" session context?", promptContext, true) {
   403→			// User declined - don't add trailer
   404→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: user declined trailer",
   405→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   406→				slog.String("source", source),
   407→			)
   408→			return nil
   409→		}
   410→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
   411→	case source == "message":
   412→		// Restoring existing checkpoint ID (amend, split commit, or non-interactive)
   413→		// No confirmation needed — data is already condensed
   414→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
   415→	default:
   416→		// Normal editor flow: add trailer with explanatory comment (will be stripped by git)
   417→		message = addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message, checkpointID, string(agentType), displayPrompt)
   418→	}
   419→
   420→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer added",
   421→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   422→		slog.String("source", source),
   423→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   424→		slog.Bool("has_new_content", hasNewContent),
   425→	)
   426→
   427→	// Write updated message back
   428→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   429→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   430→	}
   431→
   432→	return nil
   433→}
   434→
   435→// handleAmendCommitMsg handles the prepare-commit-msg hook for amend operations
   436→// (source="commit"). It preserves existing trailers or restores from PendingCheckpointID.
   437→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string) error {
   438→	// Read current commit message
   439→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   440→	if err != nil {
   441→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	message := string(content)
   445→
   446→	// If message already has a trailer, keep it unchanged
   447→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   448→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: amend preserves existing trailer",
   449→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   450→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   451→		)
   452→		return nil
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	// No trailer in message — check if any session has PendingCheckpointID to restore
   456→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   457→	if err != nil {
   458→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   459→	}
   460→
   461→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   462→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   463→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No sessions - nothing to restore
   464→	}
   465→
   466→	// Find first session with PendingCheckpointID
   467→	for _, state := range sessions {
   468→		if state.PendingCheckpointID == "" {
   469→			continue
   470→		}
   471→
   472→		cpID, err := id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID)
   473→		if err != nil {
   474→			continue // Invalid ID, skip
   475→		}
   476→
   477→		// Restore the trailer
   478→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
   479→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   480→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   481→		}
   482→
   483→		logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: restored trailer on amend from PendingCheckpointID",
   484→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   485→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   486→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   487→		)
   488→		return nil
   489→	}
   490→
   491→	// No PendingCheckpointID found - leave message unchanged
   492→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: amend with no pending checkpoint to restore",
   493→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   494→	)
   495→	return nil
   496→}
   497→
   498→// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   499→// Uses the session state machine to determine what action to take per session:
   500→//   - ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED: defer condensation (agent still working)
   501→//   - IDLE → condense immediately
   502→//   - ACTIVE_COMMITTED → migrate shadow branch (additional commit during same turn)
   503→//   - ENDED → condense if files touched, discard if empty
   504→//
   505→// Shadow branches are only deleted when ALL sessions sharing the branch are non-active.
   506→// During rebase/cherry-pick/revert operations, phase transitions are skipped entirely.
   507→//
   508→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   509→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   510→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   511→
   512→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   513→	if err != nil {
   514→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   518→	head, err := repo.Head()
   519→	if err != nil {
   520→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   524→	if err != nil {
   525→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   526→	}
   527→
   528→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   529→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   530→	if !found {
   531→		// No trailer - user removed it, treat as manual commit
   532→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no checkpoint trailer",
   533→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   534→		)
   535→		return nil
   536→	}
   537→
   538→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   539→	if err != nil {
   540→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   541→	}
   542→
   543→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   544→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   545→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   546→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   547→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   548→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   549→		)
   550→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   551→	}
   552→
   553→	// Build transition context
   554→	isRebase := isGitSequenceOperation()
   555→	transitionCtx := session.TransitionContext{
   556→		IsRebaseInProgress: isRebase,
   557→	}
   558→
   559→	if isRebase {
   560→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: rebase/sequence in progress, skipping phase transitions",
   561→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   562→		)
   563→	}
   564→
   565→	// Track shadow branch names and whether they can be deleted
   566→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   567→	// Track sessions that are still active AFTER transitions
   568→	activeSessionsOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)
   569→
   570→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   571→
   572→	// Two-pass processing: condensation first, migration second.
   573→	// This prevents a migration from renaming a shadow branch before another
   574→	// session sharing that branch has had a chance to condense from it.
   575→	type pendingMigration struct {
   576→		state *SessionState
   577→	}
   578→	var pendingMigrations []pendingMigration
   579→
   580→	// Pass 1: Run transitions and dispatch condensation/discard actions.
   581→	// Defer migration actions to pass 2.
   582→	for _, state := range sessions {
   583→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   584→
   585→		// Check for new content (needed for TransitionContext and condensation).
   586→		// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content exists so we
   587→		// don't silently skip data that should have been condensed.
   588→		hasNew, contentErr := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   589→		if contentErr != nil {
   590→			hasNew = true
   591→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: error checking session content, assuming new content",
   592→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   593→				slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()),
   594→			)
   595→		}
   596→		transitionCtx.HasFilesTouched = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   597→
   598→		// Run the state machine transition
   599→		remaining := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventGitCommit, transitionCtx)
   600→
   601→		// Dispatch strategy-specific actions.
   602→		// Each branch handles its own BaseCommit update so there is no
   603→		// fallthrough conditional at the end. On condensation failure,
   604→		// BaseCommit is intentionally NOT updated to preserve access to
   605→		// the shadow branch (which is named after the old BaseCommit).
   606→		for _, action := range remaining {
   607→			switch action {
   608→			case session.ActionCondense:
   609→				if hasNew {
   610→					s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
   611→					// condenseAndUpdateState updates BaseCommit on success.
   612→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved so the shadow branch remains accessible.
   613→				} else {
   614→					// No new content to condense — just update BaseCommit
   615→					s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   616→				}
   617→			case session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
   618→				// The state machine already gates this action on HasFilesTouched,
   619→				// but hasNew is an additional content-level check (transcript has
   620→				// new content beyond what was previously condensed).
   621→				if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew {
   622→					s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
   623→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved (same as ActionCondense).
   624→				} else {
   625→					s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   626→				}
   627→			case session.ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   628→				if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   629→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",
   630→						slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   631→					)
   632→				}
   633→				s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   634→			case session.ActionMigrateShadowBranch:
   635→				// Deferred to pass 2 so condensation reads the old shadow branch first.
   636→				// Migration updates BaseCommit as part of the rename.
   637→				// Store checkpointID so HandleTurnEnd can reuse it for deferred condensation.
   638→				state.PendingCheckpointID = checkpointID.String()
   639→				pendingMigrations = append(pendingMigrations, pendingMigration{state: state})
   640→			case session.ActionClearEndedAt, session.ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   641→				// Handled by session.ApplyCommonActions above
   642→			case session.ActionWarnStaleSession:
   643→				// Not produced by EventGitCommit; listed for switch exhaustiveness
   644→			}
   645→		}
   646→
   647→		// Save the updated state
   648→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   649→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   650→		}
   651→
   652→		// Track whether any session on this shadow branch is still active
   653→		if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   654→			activeSessionsOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   655→		}
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	// Pass 2: Run deferred migrations now that all condensations are complete.
   659→	for _, pm := range pendingMigrations {
   660→		if _, migErr := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, pm.state); migErr != nil {
   661→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: shadow branch migration failed",
   662→				slog.String("session_id", pm.state.SessionID),
   663→				slog.String("error", migErr.Error()),
   664→			)
   665→		}
   666→		// Save the migrated state
   667→		if err := s.saveSessionState(pm.state); err != nil {
   668→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state after migration: %v\n", err)
   669→		}
   670→	}
   671→
   672→	// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when ALL sessions on the branch are non-active
   673→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
   674→		if activeSessionsOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
   675→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: preserving shadow branch (active session exists)",
   676→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   677→			)
   678→			continue
   679→		}
   680→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   681→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   682→		} else {
   683→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   684→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   685→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   686→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   687→			)
   688→		}
   689→	}
   690→
   691→	return nil
   692→}
   693→
   694→// condenseAndUpdateState runs condensation for a session and updates state afterward.
   695→// Returns true if condensation succeeded.
   696→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   697→	logCtx context.Context,
   698→	repo *git.Repository,
   699→	checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   700→	state *SessionState,
   701→	head *plumbing.Reference,
   702→	shadowBranchName string,
   703→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
   704→) {
   705→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state)
   706→	if err != nil {
   707→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   708→			state.SessionID, err)
   709→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: condensation failed",
   710→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   711→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   712→		)
   713→		return
   714→	}
   715→
   716→	// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   717→	shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
   718→
   719→	// Update session state for the new base commit
   720→	state.BaseCommit = head.Hash().String()
   721→	state.StepCount = 0
   722→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   723→
   724→	// Clear attribution tracking — condensation already used these values
   725→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   726→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   727→
   728→	// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits can reuse it
   729→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   730→	// Set PendingCheckpointID for amend trailer restoration
   731→	state.PendingCheckpointID = checkpointID.String()
   732→
   733→	shortID := state.SessionID
   734→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   735→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   736→	}
   737→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   738→		shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   739→
   740→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
   741→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   742→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   743→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   744→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
   745→	)
   746→}
   747→
   748→// updateBaseCommitIfChanged updates BaseCommit to newHead if it changed.
   749→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState, newHead string) {
   750→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   751→		state.BaseCommit = newHead
   752→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updated BaseCommit",
   753→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   754→			slog.String("new_head", newHead[:7]),
   755→		)
   756→	}
   757→}
   758→
   759→// filterSessionsWithNewContent returns sessions that have new transcript content
   760→// beyond what was already condensed.
   761→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo *git.Repository, sessions []*SessionState) []*SessionState {
   762→	var result []*SessionState
   763→
   764→	for _, state := range sessions {
   765→		hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   766→		if err != nil {
   767→			// On error, include the session (fail open for hooks)
   768→			result = append(result, state)
   769→			continue
   770→		}
   771→		if hasNew {
   772→			result = append(result, state)
   773→		}
   774→	}
   775→
   776→	return result
   777→}
   778→
   779→// sessionHasNewContent checks if a session has new transcript content
   780→// beyond what was already condensed.
   781→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContent(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   782→	// Get shadow branch
   783→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   784→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   785→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   786→	if err != nil {
   787→		// No shadow branch means no Stop has happened since the last condensation.
   788→		// However, the agent may have done work (including commits) without a Stop.
   789→		// Check the live transcript to detect this scenario.
   790→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   791→	}
   792→
   793→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   794→	if err != nil {
   795→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   796→	}
   797→
   798→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   799→	if err != nil {
   800→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   801→	}
   802→
   803→	// Look for transcript file
   804→	metadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + state.SessionID
   805→	var transcriptLines int
   806→
   807→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   808→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   809→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptLines(content)
   810→		}
   811→	} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   812→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   813→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptLines(content)
   814→		}
   815→	}
   816→
   817→	// Has new content if there are more lines than already condensed
   818→	return transcriptLines > state.CheckpointTranscriptStart, nil
   819→}
   820→
   821→// countTranscriptLines counts lines in a transcript, matching the counting method used
   822→// in extractSessionData for consistency. This trims trailing empty lines (from final \n
   823→// in JSONL) but includes empty lines in the middle of the file.
   824→func countTranscriptLines(content string) int {
   825→	lines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   826→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   827→	for len(lines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(lines[len(lines)-1]) == "" {
   828→		lines = lines[:len(lines)-1]
   829→	}
   830→	return len(lines)
   831→}
   832→
   833→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
   834→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
   835→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
   836→//
   837→// Returns true if:
   838→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
   839→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
   840→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
   841→//
   842→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
   843→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
   844→//
   845→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
   846→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   847→	// Need both transcript path and agent type to analyze
   848→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
   849→		return false, nil
   850→	}
   851→
   852→	// Get the agent for transcript analysis
   853→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
   854→	if err != nil {
   855→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Unknown agent type, fail gracefully
   856→	}
   857→
   858→	// Cast to TranscriptAnalyzer
   859→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
   860→	if !ok {
   861→		return false, nil // Agent doesn't support transcript analysis
   862→	}
   863→
   864→	// Get current transcript position
   865→	currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)
   866→	if err != nil {
   867→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error reading transcript, fail gracefully
   868→	}
   869→
   870→	// Check if transcript has grown since last condensation
   871→	if currentPos <= state.CheckpointTranscriptStart {
   872→		return false, nil // No new content
   873→	}
   874→
   875→	// Transcript has grown - check if there are file modifications in the new portion
   876→	modifiedFiles, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   877→	if err != nil {
   878→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error parsing transcript, fail gracefully
   879→	}
   880→
   881→	// No file modifications means no new content to checkpoint
   882→	if len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
   883→		return false, nil
   884→	}
   885→
   886→	// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files
   887→	// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include
   888→	// files the agent actually modified
   889→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo) // TODO: does it work? Do we have staged files if this is called at post-commit hook ?
   890→	if !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {
   891→		return false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work
   892→	}
   893→
   894→	return true, nil
   895→}
   896→
   897→// addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
   898→// Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
   899→func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
   900→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   901→
   902→	// If message already ends with trailers (lines starting with key:), just append
   903→	// Otherwise, add a blank line first
   904→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimRight(message, "\n"), "\n")
   905→
   906→	// Check if last non-empty, non-comment line looks like a trailer
   907→	// Git comment lines start with # and may contain ": " (e.g., "# Changes to be committed:")
   908→	hasTrailers := false
   909→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   910→		line := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
   911→		if line == "" {
   912→			break
   913→		}
   914→		// Skip git comment lines
   915→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
   916→			continue
   917→		}
   918→		if strings.Contains(line, ": ") {
   919→			hasTrailers = true
   920→			break
   921→		}
   922→		// Non-comment, non-trailer line found - no existing trailers
   923→		break
   924→	}
   925→
   926→	if hasTrailers {
   927→		// Append trailer directly
   928→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n" + trailer + "\n"
   929→	}
   930→
   931→	// Add blank line before trailer
   932→	return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n"
   933→}
   934→
   935→// addCheckpointTrailerWithComment adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer with an explanatory comment.
   936→// The trailer is placed above the git comment block but below the user's message area,
   937→// with a comment explaining that the user can remove it if they don't want to link the commit
   938→// to the agent session. If prompt is non-empty, it's shown as context.
   939→func addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, agentName, prompt string) string {
   940→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   941→	commentLines := []string{
   942→		"# Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer above if you don't want to link this commit to " + agentName + " session context.",
   943→	}
   944→	if prompt != "" {
   945→		commentLines = append(commentLines, "# Last Prompt: "+prompt)
   946→	}
   947→	commentLines = append(commentLines, "# The trailer will be added to your next commit based on this branch.")
   948→	comment := strings.Join(commentLines, "\n")
   949→
   950→	lines := strings.Split(message, "\n")
   951→
   952→	// Find where the git comment block starts (first # line)
   953→	commentStart := -1
   954→	for i, line := range lines {
   955→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
   956→			commentStart = i
   957→			break
   958→		}
   959→	}
   960→
   961→	if commentStart == -1 {
   962→		// No git comments, append trailer at the end
   963→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n"
   964→	}
   965→
   966→	// Split into user content and git comments
   967→	userContent := strings.Join(lines[:commentStart], "\n")
   968→	gitComments := strings.Join(lines[commentStart:], "\n")
   969→
   970→	// Build result: user content, blank line, trailer, comment, blank line, git comments
   971→	userContent = strings.TrimRight(userContent, "\n")
   972→	if userContent == "" {
   973→		// No user content yet - leave space for them to type, then trailer
   974→		// Two newlines: first for user's message line, second for blank separator
   975→		return "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
   976→	}
   977→	return userContent + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
   978→}
   979→
   980→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session or updates an existing one.
   981→// This implements the optional SessionInitializer interface.
   982→// Called during UserPromptSubmit to allow git hooks to detect active sessions.
   983→//
   984→// If the session already exists and HEAD has moved (e.g., user committed), updates
   985→// BaseCommit to the new HEAD so future checkpoints go to the correct shadow branch.
   986→//
   987→// If there's an existing shadow branch with commits from a different session ID,
   988→// returns a SessionIDConflictError to prevent orphaning existing session work.
   989→//
   990→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   991→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   992→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   993→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
   994→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   995→	if err != nil {
   996→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   997→	}
   998→
   999→	// Check if session already exists
  1000→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1001→	if err != nil {
  1002→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check session state: %w", err)
  1003→	}
  1004→
  1005→	if state != nil && state.BaseCommit != "" {
  1006→		// Session is fully initialized — apply phase transition for TurnStart
  1007→		TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{})
  1008→
  1009→		// Backfill AgentType if empty or set to the generic default "Agent"
  1010→		if !isSpecificAgentType(state.AgentType) && agentType != "" {
  1011→			state.AgentType = agentType
  1012→		}
  1013→
  1014→		// Backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions created before the first_prompt field was added)
  1015→		if state.FirstPrompt == "" && userPrompt != "" {
  1016→			state.FirstPrompt = truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt)
  1017→		}
  1018→
  1019→		// Update transcript path if provided (may change on session resume)
  1020→		if transcriptPath != "" && state.TranscriptPath != transcriptPath {
  1021→			state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath
  1022→		}
  1023→
  1024→		// Clear checkpoint IDs on every new prompt
  1025→		// These are set during PostCommit when a checkpoint is created, and should be
  1026→		// cleared when the user enters a new prompt (starting fresh work)
  1027→		if state.LastCheckpointID != "" {
  1028→			state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1029→		}
  1030→		if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
  1031→			state.PendingCheckpointID = ""
  1032→		}
  1033→
  1034→		// Calculate attribution at prompt start (BEFORE agent makes any changes)
  1035→		// This captures user edits since the last checkpoint (or base commit for first prompt).
  1036→		// IMPORTANT: Always calculate attribution, even for the first checkpoint, to capture
  1037→		// user edits made before the first prompt. The inner CalculatePromptAttribution handles
  1038→		// nil lastCheckpointTree by falling back to baseTree.
  1039→		promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1040→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1041→
  1042→		// Check if HEAD has moved (user pulled/rebased or committed)
  1043→		// migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded handles renaming the shadow branch and updating state.BaseCommit
  1044→		if _, err := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
  1045→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check/migrate shadow branch: %w", err)
  1046→		}
  1047→
  1048→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1049→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
  1050→		}
  1051→		return nil
  1052→	}
  1053→	// If state exists but BaseCommit is empty, it's a partial state from concurrent warning
  1054→	// Continue below to properly initialize it
  1055→
  1056→	// Initialize new session
  1057→	state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath, userPrompt)
  1058→	if err != nil {
  1059→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
  1060→	}
  1061→
  1062→	// Apply phase transition: new session starts as ACTIVE
  1063→	TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{})
  1064→
  1065→	// Calculate attribution for pre-prompt edits
  1066→	// This captures any user edits made before the first prompt
  1067→	promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1068→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1069→	if err = s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1070→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save attribution: %w", err)
  1071→	}
  1072→
  1073→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Initialized shadow session: %s\n", sessionID)
  1074→	return nil
  1075→}
  1076→
  1077→// calculatePromptAttributionAtStart calculates attribution at prompt start (before agent runs).
  1078→// This captures user changes since the last checkpoint - no filtering needed since
  1079→// the agent hasn't made any changes yet.
  1080→//
  1081→// IMPORTANT: This reads from the worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1082→// captures in checkpoints. If we read staged content but checkpoints capture worktree content,
  1083→// unstaged changes would be in the checkpoint but not counted in PromptAttribution, causing
  1084→// them to be incorrectly attributed to the agent later.
  1085→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(
  1086→	repo *git.Repository,
  1087→	state *SessionState,
  1088→) PromptAttribution {
  1089→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
  1090→	nextCheckpointNum := state.StepCount + 1
  1091→	result := PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: nextCheckpointNum}
  1092→
  1093→	// Get last checkpoint tree from shadow branch (if it exists)
  1094→	// For the first checkpoint, no shadow branch exists yet - this is fine,
  1095→	// CalculatePromptAttribution will use baseTree as the reference instead.
  1096→	var lastCheckpointTree *object.Tree
  1097→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1098→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1099→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1100→	if err != nil {
  1101→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: no shadow branch yet (first checkpoint)",
  1102→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName))
  1103→		// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1104→	} else {
  1105→		shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1106→		if err != nil {
  1107→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow commit",
  1108→				slog.String("shadow_ref", ref.Hash().String()),
  1109→				slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1110→			// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1111→		} else {
  1112→			lastCheckpointTree, err = shadowCommit.Tree()
  1113→			if err != nil {
  1114→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow tree",
  1115→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1116→				// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1117→			}
  1118→		}
  1119→	}
  1120→
  1121→	// Get base tree for agent lines calculation
  1122→	var baseTree *object.Tree
  1123→	if baseCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(state.BaseCommit)); err == nil {
  1124→		if tree, treeErr := baseCommit.Tree(); treeErr == nil {
  1125→			baseTree = tree
  1126→		} else {
  1127→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base tree unavailable",
  1128→				slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
  1129→		}
  1130→	} else {
  1131→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base commit unavailable",
  1132→			slog.String("base_commit", state.BaseCommit),
  1133→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1134→	}
  1135→
  1136→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1137→	if err != nil {
  1138→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree",
  1139→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1140→		return result
  1141→	}
  1142→
  1143→	// Get worktree status to find ALL changed files
  1144→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1145→	if err != nil {
  1146→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree status",
  1147→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1148→		return result
  1149→	}
  1150→
  1151→	worktreeRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
  1152→
  1153→	// Build map of changed files with their worktree content
  1154→	// IMPORTANT: We read from worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1155→	// captures in checkpoints. This ensures attribution is consistent.
  1156→	changedFiles := make(map[string]string)
  1157→	for filePath, fileStatus := range status {
  1158→		// Skip unmodified files
  1159→		if fileStatus.Worktree == git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging == git.Unmodified {
  1160→			continue
  1161→		}
  1162→		// Skip .entire metadata directory (session data, not user code)
  1163→		if strings.HasPrefix(filePath, paths.EntireMetadataDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(filePath, ".entire/") {
  1164→			continue
  1165→		}
  1166→
  1167→		// Always read from worktree to match checkpoint behavior
  1168→		fullPath := filepath.Join(worktreeRoot, filePath)
  1169→		var content string
  1170→		if data, err := os.ReadFile(fullPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // filePath is from git worktree status
  1171→			// Use git's binary detection algorithm (matches getFileContent behavior).
  1172→			// Binary files are excluded from line-based attribution calculations.
  1173→			isBinary, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(data))
  1174→			if binErr == nil && !isBinary {
  1175→				content = string(data)
  1176→			}
  1177→		}
  1178→		// else: file deleted, unreadable, or binary - content remains empty string
  1179→
  1180→		changedFiles[filePath] = content
  1181→	}
  1182→
  1183→	// Use CalculatePromptAttribution from manual_commit_attribution.go
  1184→	result = CalculatePromptAttribution(baseTree, lastCheckpointTree, changedFiles, nextCheckpointNum)
  1185→
  1186→	return result
  1187→}
  1188→
  1189→// getStagedFiles returns a list of files staged for commit.
  1190→func getStagedFiles(repo *git.Repository) []string {
  1191→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1192→	if err != nil {
  1193→		return nil
  1194→	}
  1195→
  1196→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1197→	if err != nil {
  1198→		return nil
  1199→	}
  1200→
  1201→	var staged []string
  1202→	for path, fileStatus := range status {
  1203→		// Check if file is staged (in index)
  1204→		if fileStatus.Staging != git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging != git.Untracked {
  1205→			staged = append(staged, path)
  1206→		}
  1207→	}
  1208→	return staged
  1209→}
  1210→
  1211→// getLastPrompt retrieves the most recent user prompt from a session's shadow branch.
  1212→// Returns empty string if no prompt can be retrieved.
  1213→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getLastPrompt(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) string {
  1214→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1215→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1216→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1217→	if err != nil {
  1218→		return ""
  1219→	}
  1220→
  1221→	// Extract session data to get prompts for commit message generation
  1222→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini)
  1223→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, nil, state.AgentType, "")
  1224→	if err != nil || len(sessionData.Prompts) == 0 {
  1225→		return ""
  1226→	}
  1227→
  1228→	// Return the last prompt (most recent work before commit)
  1229→	return sessionData.Prompts[len(sessionData.Prompts)-1]
  1230→}
  1231→
  1232→// HandleTurnEnd dispatches strategy-specific actions emitted when an agent turn ends.
  1233→// This handles the ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE transition where ActionCondense is deferred
  1234→// from PostCommit (agent was still active during the commit).
  1235→//
  1236→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
  1237→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HandleTurnEnd(state *SessionState, actions []session.Action) error {
  1238→	if len(actions) == 0 {
  1239→		return nil
  1240→	}
  1241→
  1242→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1243→
  1244→	for _, action := range actions {
  1245→		switch action {
  1246→		case session.ActionCondense:
  1247→			s.handleTurnEndCondense(logCtx, state)
  1248→		case session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, session.ActionDiscardIfNoFiles,
  1249→			session.ActionMigrateShadowBranch, session.ActionWarnStaleSession:
  1250→			// Not expected at turn-end; log for diagnostics.
  1251→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "turn-end: unexpected action",
  1252→				slog.String("action", action.String()),
  1253→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1254→			)
  1255→		case session.ActionClearEndedAt, session.ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
  1256→			// Handled by session.ApplyCommonActions before this is called.
  1257→		}
  1258→	}
  1259→	return nil
  1260→}
  1261→
  1262→// handleTurnEndCondense performs deferred condensation at turn end.
  1263→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) handleTurnEndCondense(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState) {
  1264→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1265→	if err != nil {
  1266→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "turn-end condense: failed to open repo",
  1267→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1268→		return
  1269→	}
  1270→
  1271→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1272→	if err != nil {
  1273→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "turn-end condense: failed to get HEAD",
  1274→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1275→		return
  1276→	}
  1277→
  1278→	// Derive checkpoint ID from PendingCheckpointID (set during PostCommit),
  1279→	// or generate a new one if not set.
  1280→	var checkpointID id.CheckpointID
  1281→	if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
  1282→		if cpID, parseErr := id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID); parseErr == nil {
  1283→			checkpointID = cpID
  1284→		}
  1285→	}
  1286→	if checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
  1287→		cpID, genErr := id.Generate()
  1288→		if genErr != nil {
  1289→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "turn-end condense: failed to generate checkpoint ID",
  1290→				slog.String("error", genErr.Error()))
  1291→			return
  1292→		}
  1293→		checkpointID = cpID
  1294→	}
  1295→
  1296→	// Check if there is actually new content to condense.
  1297→	// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content so we don't silently skip.
  1298→	hasNew, contentErr := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
  1299→	if contentErr != nil {
  1300→		hasNew = true
  1301→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "turn-end condense: error checking content, assuming new",
  1302→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1303→			slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()))
  1304→	}
  1305→
  1306→	if !hasNew {
  1307→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "turn-end condense: no new content",
  1308→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
  1309→		return
  1310→	}
  1311→
  1312→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1313→	shadowBranchesToDelete := map[string]struct{}{}
  1314→
  1315→	s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
  1316→
  1317→	// Delete shadow branches after condensation — but only if no other active
  1318→	// sessions share the branch (same safety check PostCommit uses).
  1319→	for branchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
  1320→		if s.hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch(state.SessionID, state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID) {
  1321→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "turn-end: preserving shadow branch (other active session exists)",
  1322→				slog.String("shadow_branch", branchName))
  1323→			continue
  1324→		}
  1325→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, branchName); err != nil {
  1326→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", branchName, err)
  1327→		} else {
  1328→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", branchName)
  1329→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted (turn-end)",
  1330→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
  1331→				slog.String("shadow_branch", branchName),
  1332→			)
  1333→		}
  1334→	}
  1335→}
  1336→
  1337→// hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch checks if any other sessions with the same
  1338→// base commit and worktree ID are in an active phase. Used to prevent deleting
  1339→// a shadow branch that another session still needs.
  1340→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch(currentSessionID, baseCommit, worktreeID string) bool {
  1341→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForCommit(baseCommit)
  1342→	if err != nil {
  1343→		return false // Fail-open: if we can't check, don't block deletion
  1344→	}
  1345→	for _, other := range sessions {
  1346→		if other.SessionID == currentSessionID {
  1347→			continue
  1348→		}
  1349→		if other.WorktreeID == worktreeID && other.Phase.IsActive() {
  1350→			return true
  1351→		}
  1352→	}
  1353→	return false
  1354→}
  1355→
  1356→// hasOverlappingFiles checks if any file in stagedFiles appears in filesTouched.
  1357→func hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, filesTouched []string) bool {
  1358→	touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1359→	for _, f := range filesTouched {
  1360→		touchedSet[f] = true
  1361→	}
  1362→
  1363→	for _, staged := range stagedFiles {
  1364→		if touchedSet[staged] {
  1365→			return true
  1366→		}
  1367→	}
  1368→	return false
  1369→}
  1370→

    21→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation verifies that PostCommit on an
    22→// ACTIVE session transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED without condensing.
    23→// The shadow branch must be preserved because the session is still active.
    24→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation(t *testing.T) {
    25→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    26→	t.Chdir(dir)
    27→
    28→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    29→	require.NoError(t, err)
    30→
    31→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    32→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-active"
    33→
    34→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
    35→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
    36→
    37→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)
    38→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    39→	require.NoError(t, err)
    40→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
    41→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    42→
    43→	// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer on the main branch
    44→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
    45→
    46→	// Run PostCommit
    47→	err = s.PostCommit()
    48→	require.NoError(t, err)
    49→
    50→	// Verify phase transitioned to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    51→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    52→	require.NoError(t, err)
    53→	require.NotNil(t, state)
    54→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
    55→		"ACTIVE session should transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED on GitCommit")
    56→
    57→	// Verify shadow branch is NOT deleted (session is still active).
    58→	// After PostCommit, BaseCommit is updated to new HEAD, so use the current state.
    59→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    60→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    61→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
    62→	assert.NoError(t, err,
    63→		"shadow branch should be preserved when session is still active")
    64→}

   299→	// Phase transition still applies even when condensation fails

   302→}

   366→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses verifies that an ENDED
   367→// session with files touched and new content condenses on commit.
   368→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses(t *testing.T) {

   447→
   448→	// Record shadow branch name and original StepCount
   449→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   450→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   451→
   452→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   453→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a2")
   454→
   455→	// Get new HEAD hash
   456→	head, err := repo.Head()
   457→	require.NoError(t, err)
   458→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   459→
   460→	// Run PostCommit
   461→	err = s.PostCommit()
   462→	require.NoError(t, err)
   463→
   464→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (no condensation)
   465→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   466→	require.Error(t, err,
   467→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when no new content")
   468→
   469→	// Shadow branch should still exist
   470→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   471→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   472→	require.NoError(t, err,
   473→		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
   474→
   475→	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough
   476→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   477→	require.NoError(t, err)
   478→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   479→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough")
   480→
   481→	// StepCount unchanged
   482→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   483→		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
   484→}
   485→
   486→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards verifies that an ENDED
   487→// session with no files touched takes the discard path, updating BaseCommit.
   488→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards(t *testing.T) {
   489→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   490→	t.Chdir(dir)
   491→
   492→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   493→	require.NoError(t, err)
   494→
   495→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   496→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-discard"
   497→
   498→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   499→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   500→
   501→	// Set phase to ENDED with no files touched
   502→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   503→	require.NoError(t, err)
   504→	now := time.Now()
   505→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   506→	state.EndedAt = &now
   507→	state.FilesTouched = nil // No files touched
   508→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   509→
   510→	// Record original StepCount
   511→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   512→
   513→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   514→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b3")
   515→
   516→	// Get new HEAD hash
   517→	head, err := repo.Head()
   518→	require.NoError(t, err)
   519→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   520→
   521→	// Run PostCommit
   522→	err = s.PostCommit()
   523→	require.NoError(t, err)
   524→
   525→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (no condensation for discard path)
   526→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   527→	require.Error(t, err,
   528→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist for discard path")
   529→
   530→	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD
   531→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   532→	require.NoError(t, err)
   533→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   534→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD on discard path")
   535→
   536→	// StepCount unchanged
   537→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   538→		"StepCount should be unchanged on discard path")
   539→
   540→	// Phase stays ENDED
   541→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   542→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED on discard path")
   543→}

   545→// TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch verifies that an
   546→// ACTIVE_COMMITTED session receiving another commit migrates the shadow branch
   547→// to the new HEAD and stays in ACTIVE_COMMITTED.
   548→func TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   549→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   550→	t.Chdir(dir)
   551→
   552→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   553→	require.NoError(t, err)
   554→
   555→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   556→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ac-migrate"
   557→
   558→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   559→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   560→
   561→	// Set phase to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   562→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   563→	require.NoError(t, err)
   564→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted
   565→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   566→
   567→	// Record original shadow branch name and BaseCommit
   568→	originalShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   569→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   570→
   571→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   572→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c4")
   573→
   574→	// Run PostCommit
   575→	err = s.PostCommit()
   576→	require.NoError(t, err)
   577→
   578→	// Verify phase stays ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   579→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   580→	require.NoError(t, err)
   581→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
   582→		"ACTIVE_COMMITTED session should stay ACTIVE_COMMITTED on subsequent commit")
   583→
   584→	// Verify BaseCommit updated to new HEAD
   585→	head, err := repo.Head()
   586→	require.NoError(t, err)
   587→	assert.Equal(t, head.Hash().String(), state.BaseCommit,
   588→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD after migration")
   589→
   590→	// Verify new shadow branch exists at new HEAD hash
   591→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   592→	newRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   593→	_, err = repo.Reference(newRefName, true)
   594→	require.NoError(t, err,
   595→		"new shadow branch should exist after migration")
   596→
   597→	// Verify original shadow branch no longer exists (was migrated/renamed)
   598→	oldRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(originalShadowBranch)
   599→	_, err = repo.Reference(oldRefName, true)
   600→	require.Error(t, err,
   601→		"original shadow branch should no longer exist after migration")
   602→
   603→	// StepCount unchanged (no condensation)
   604→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   605→		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   606→
   607→	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
   608→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   609→	require.Error(t, err,
   610→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   611→}
